We are solving the Game of 24. We are given four numbers: 2, 5, 7, 7. We need to produce valid arithmetic expression using all four numbers exactly once, using only +, -, *, / and the expression must evaluate to exactly 24. We need to produce a final answer in \boxed{...}.
